If `f7g6+ Kg8`, then the king is at g8 and the rook on e7 is NOT pinned.

- If `...Kg8`, then the king is at g8.

On e6: attacks g8 (wait, the king is on g8), f5, d5, c4, b3, a2.
On g6: attacks e8, f5, e4, d3, c2, b1, and h5, f7, e8.
Wait, a bishop on g6 attacks the square e8.
A bishop on e6 attacks the square g8.
Since the king is likely to end up on either e8 or g8, the bishop is useful on either square.
